```
// 内部 tile / wall 注册表。id 即数组下标。
// 数值（硬度/光照）为原创平衡设计；mapColor 参考 TEdit tiles.json 的地图配色后重新取整。

export interface TileDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  solid: boolean;        // 参与碰撞
  platform: boolean;     // 单向平台（下跳可穿）
  decor: boolean;        // 装饰物（不碰撞、可被任何工具秒清）
  framed: boolean;       // 多格框架物体（使用 frameX/frameY 定位贴图）
  opacity: number;       // 光照阻挡 0-1
  light?: [number, number, number]; // 自发光 RGB
  pick: number;          // 所需镐力（-1 不可挖）
  axe: number;           // 所需斧力
  mapColor: string;      // 小地图颜色
  drop?: string | number | null;   // 破坏掉落:数字=原版 item id(2026-08 本地物品退役后统一);
                                   // 字符串=place_v_ 派生等存量 key;null = 显式无掉落,原版装饰类
  rope?: boolean;        // 绳索类（攀爬判定）
  blend: boolean;        // 是否参与同类边缘融合
  attach?: 'ground' | 'wall'; // 装饰物附着需求
  w?: number; h?: number;     // framed 物体占格数
  vanilla?: {                 // 原版素材渲染（terraria-assets + TEdit 数据）
    sheet: number;            // Tiles_N 表 id
    frame: 'auto' | 'style' | 'blend'; // auto=47 表 8 向；style=显式帧；blend=BlendRules 合并分帧（泥土/石/草族）
    fw?: number; fh?: number; // style 类的占格数（TEdit frameSize）
  };
}

export const T = {
  EMPTY: 0, DIRT: 1, STONE: 2, GRASS: 3,
  ORE_COPPER: 4, ORE_IRON: 5, ORE_SILVER: 6, ORE_GOLD: 7,
  TREE: 8, LEAVES: 9, WOOD: 10, PLATFORM: 11,
  TORCH: 12, WORKBENCH: 13, FURNACE: 14, ANVIL: 15,
  CHEST: 16, DOOR_CLOSED: 17, DOOR_OPEN: 18,
  MUSHROOM: 19, FLOWER: 20, TALLGRASS: 21, SAND: 22, SNOW: 23,
  SAPLING: 24, ASH: 25,
} as const;

export const TILE_DEFS: TileDef[] = [];
function def(d: Partial<TileDef> & { key: string }): number {
  const id = TILE_DEFS.length;
  TILE_DEFS.push({
    name: d.key, solid: false, platform: false, decor: false, framed: false,
    opacity: 0, pick: -1, axe: -1, mapColor: '#000', blend: false, ...d, id,
  } as TileDef);
  return id;
}
// 保证 id 与 T 常量一致（按顺序注册）
def({ key: 'empty', name: '空气' });
def({ key: 'dirt', name: '泥土', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#976B4B', blend: true, drop: 2, vanilla: { sheet: 0, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'stone', name: '石块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#808080', blend: true, drop: 3, vanilla: { sheet: 1, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'grass', name: '草块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#1CD85E', blend: true, drop: 2, vanilla: { sheet: 2, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'ore_copper', name: '铜矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#964316', blend: false, drop: 12, vanilla: { sheet: 7, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_iron', name: '铁矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#8C6550', blend: false, drop: 11, vanilla: { sheet: 6, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_silver', name: '银矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 20, mapColor: '#B9C2C3', blend: false, drop: 14, vanilla: { sheet: 9, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'ore_gold', name: '金矿', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 40, mapColor: '#B9A417', blend: false, drop: 13, vanilla: { sheet: 8, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'tree', name: '树干', solid: false, framed: true, opacity: 0.1, axe: 0, mapColor: '#976B4B', drop: 9, vanilla: { sheet: 5, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 1 } });
def({ key: 'leaves', name: '树叶', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, axe: 0, mapColor: '#2E8B3D', drop: null });
def({ key: 'wood', name: '木材块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#976B4B', blend: true, drop: 9, vanilla: { sheet: 30, frame: 'auto' } });
def({ key: 'platform', name: '木平台', platform: true, opacity: 0.15, pick: 0, axe: 0, mapColor: '#BF8E6F', drop: 94, vanilla: { sheet: 19, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 1 } });
def({ key: 'torch', name: '火把', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0, pick: 0, light: [255, 225, 150], mapColor: '#FDDD03', drop: 8, vanilla: { sheet: 4, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 1 } });
// 注：火把光值见下（更亮版本）
def({ key: 'workbench', name: '工作台', solid: false, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, w: 2, h: 1, mapColor: '#BF8E6F', drop: 36, vanilla: { sheet: 18, frame: 'style', fw: 2, fh: 1 } });
def({ key: 'furnace', name: '熔炉', solid: false, /* 原版 tileSolid[17]=false：熔炉可通行 */ framed: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, w: 3, h: 2, light: [255, 140, 70], mapColor: '#909490', drop: 33, vanilla: { sheet: 17, frame: 'style', fw: 3, fh: 2 } });
def({ key: 'anvil', name: '铁砧', solid: false, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, w: 2, h: 1, mapColor: '#8C8274', drop: 35, vanilla: { sheet: 16, frame: 'style', fw: 2, fh: 1 } });
def({ key: 'chest', name: '宝箱', solid: false, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, w: 2, h: 2, mapColor: '#AE815C', drop: 48, vanilla: { sheet: 21, frame: 'style', fw: 2, fh: 2 } });
def({ key: 'door_closed', name: '门', solid: true, framed: true, opacity: 0.05, pick: 0, w: 1, h: 3, mapColor: '#77694F', drop: 25, vanilla: { sheet: 10, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 3 } });
def({ key: 'door_open', name: '门(开)', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0, pick: 0, w: 2, h: 3, mapColor: '#77694F', drop: 25, vanilla: { sheet: 11, frame: 'style', fw: 2, fh: 3 } });
def({ key: 'mushroom', name: '蘑菇', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0, pick: 0, mapColor: '#1AC454', drop: 5, vanilla: { sheet: 3, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 1 } });

def({ key: 'tallgrass', name: '草丛', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0, pick: 0, axe: 0, mapColor: '#1AC454', drop: 27, vanilla: { sheet: 3, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 1 } });
def({ key: 'sand', name: '沙块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#BAA854', blend: true, drop: 169, vanilla: { sheet: 53, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'snow', name: '雪块', solid: true, opacity: 1, pick: 0, mapColor: '#D3ECF1', blend: true, drop: 593, vanilla: { sheet: 147, frame: 'blend' } });
def({ key: 'acorn_sapling', name: '树苗', decor: true, framed: true, opacity: 0, pick: 0, axe: 0, mapColor: '#A37451', drop: 27, vanilla: { sheet: 20, frame: 'style', fw: 1, fh: 2 } }); // fh:2=原版 TileObjectData Width1/Height2+frameSize[1,2](2026-08-13 atlas-lint ATL-06 裁决;此前 fh:1 错)

// ===================== 原版素材图块（terraria-assets + TEdit 数据） =====================
// 4a：矿石 / 宝石 / 冰雪 / 沙漠 / 基础方块（1x1 实心，frame:'auto' 8 向贴合）
```

```
144	/** 是否笼族 sheet（CAGE_FAMILY 在库即笼族） */
145	export function cageFamilyOf(sheet: number): CageFamily | null {
146	  return CAGE_FAMILY[sheet] ?? null;
147	}
148	
149	/** 顶盖样式（TileID.cs:199；非笼族 -1） */
150	export function cageLidStyle(sheet: number): number {
151	  return CAGE_FAMILY[sheet]?.lid ?? -1;
152	}
153	
154	/** 槽位哈希（TileDrawing.cs:6639-6652 1:1；x/y = 本格坐标，fx/fy = tile 帧偏移）。
155	 *  物体锚 = (x - fx/18, y - fy/18)；big 除 6×4、small 除 3×3、water 除 2×3，
156	 *  积 mod cageFrames(25)。JS 负数取模已归正（锚不可能为负，防御性） */
157	export function cageSlot(kind: CageSlotKind, x: number, y: number, fx: number, fy: number): number {
158	  const ox = x - Math.floor(fx / 18);
159	  const oy = y - Math.floor(fy / 18);
160	  const [dx, dy] = kind === 'big' ? [6, 4] : kind === 'small' ? [3, 3] : [2, 3];
161	  return (((Math.floor(ox / dx) * Math.floor(oy / dy)) % CAGE_FRAMES) + CAGE_FRAMES) % CAGE_FRAMES;
162	}
163	
164	/** 确定性 32 位哈希（槽位/族种子化用；非密码学，只求均匀） */
165	function hash32(a: number, b: number): number {
166	  let h = (a * 0x9e3779b1) ^ (b * 0x85ebca6b);
167	  h ^= h >>> 13; h = Math.imul(h, 0xc2b2ae35); h ^= h >>> 16;
168	  return h >>> 0;
169	}
170	
171	/**
172	 * 笼内小动物动画帧行（≈ AnimateTiles_CritterCages Main.cs:13452-16530）。
173	 * 原版为逐族状态机（空闲 rand(30,900)t → 族专属动作段 5/10t 推进 → 回空闲，双空闲
174	 * 帧/循环重掷各族不同）；此处取同构确定性循环：空闲帧 0 停 [30,900)t（hash 种子，
175	 * 每 65536t 换一拍近似原版逐次重掷）→ 帧 1..frames-1 每 5t 一帧 → 回空闲。
176	 * @param family 动画族序号（CageFamily.idx）
177	 * @param slot   槽位（cageSlot）
178	 * @param tick   全局动画 tick（tileAnim.tick）
179	 * @param frames 该表帧行数（floor(表高/step)）
180	 */
181	export function cageFrameRow(family: number, slot: number, tick: number, frames: number): number {
182	  if (frames <= 1) return 0;
183	  const episode = Math.floor(tick / 65536);                       // 换拍周期
184	  const idle = 30 + hash32(family * 131 + slot, episode) % 871;   // rand.Next(30, 900)
185	  const action = (frames - 1) * ACTION_TICKS;
186	  const cycle = idle + action;
187	  const t = ((tick % cycle) + cycle) % cycle;
188	  if (t < idle) return 0;
189	  return 1 + Math.min(frames - 2, Math.floor((t - idle) / ACTION_TICKS));
190	}
191	
192	/** 重烘焙节奏（ChunkCache.advanceAnim 用）：动作帧 5t/帧 → 每 5 tick 重烘焙含笼 chunk */
193	export function cageAnimRate(): number {
194	  return ACTION_TICKS;
195	}
196	
197	export interface DrawSource { img: HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }
198	
199	/**
200	 * 笼族一格绘制（TileDrawing.cs:1670-1716 顶盖段 1:1）。
201	 * @param r       drawVanillaCell 已取的本格帧（vframeAt 结果，含 18px 步长源坐标）
202	 * @param sheetH  笼表总像素高（算帧行数）
203	 * @param tileTop 本格 tileTop（DRAW_Y_OFFSET 恒 2）
204	 */
205	export function drawCageCell(
206	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: { ensureVImage: (f: string) => HTMLImageElement | null },
207	  fam: CageFamily, r: DrawSource, x: number, y: number, fx: number, fy: number,
208	  px: number, py: number, tileTop: number, sheetH: number,
209	): void {
210	  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.floor(sheetH / fam.step));
211	  // 槽位哈希用【存储帧】（物体锚还原，TileDrawing.cs:6646-6652 同式）
212	  const slot = cageSlot(fam.slot, x, y, fx, fy);
213	  const row = cageFrameRow(fam.idx, slot, tileAnim.tick, rows);
214	  const asx = r.sx;
215	  const asy = r.sy + row * fam.step;
216	  if (asy + r.sh > r.img.height) return;  // 帧行越界（表残缺）：整格跳过防画出邻带
217	  const baseY = py + tileTop;
218	  const isTopRow = asy % fam.step === 0;  // 当前帧行首行格（:1673/:1675 的 %54/%36）
219	  if (!isTopRow || fam.lid < 0) {
220	    ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy, r.sw, r.sh, px, baseY, r.sw, r.sh);
221	    return;
222	  }
223	  // 首行格：本体下移 8px（源裁顶 8px，:1679-1686）+ 顶盖画在 tileTop-2（:1687-1694）
224	  ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8, px, baseY + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8);
225	  const lid = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/CageTop_${fam.lid}.png`);
226	  if (!lid) return;
227	  // style 0 顶盖 X 取源 X %108（剥河豚横带；:1690-1693）；宽 16 高 10（:1694-1696）
228	  const lidX = fam.lid === 0 ? asx % 108 : asx;
229	  if (lidX + 16 > lid.width) return;
230	  ctx.drawImage(lid, lidX, 0, 16, 10, px, baseY - 2, 16, 10);
231	}
232
```

```
    const aboveFull = st.inBounds(x, y - 1) && (() => {
      const j = st.idx(x, y - 1);
      return !!st.flags[j] && !!TILE_DEFS[st.type[j]]?.solid && !st.half[j] && st.slope[j] === 0;
```

````
## 3. `.wld` import mapping

File: `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/wld/WldImport.ts` (656 lines). (Its companion `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/wld/WldParser.ts` only parses the binary format, including the `tileFrameImportant` bit array at line 108.)

Two-layer mapping:

1. **`SHEET_FALLBACK`** (lines 28–48) — generic index: the *first* `TILE_DEFS` entry whose `vanilla.sheet === wldTileId` wins; `copyFrame` is set from `def.framed`. Comment at lines 28–30: "2026-08-12 tile 全量补齐后 753 个 sheet 全部有注册——任意原版存档不再降级石块".
2. **`TILE_MAP`** (lines 50–~410, 356 numeric keys) — explicit overrides, mostly `{ key, copyFrame: true }` for framed furniture.

Consumption loop at lines 456–490: `TILE_MAP[wt]` first (string / object / null-clear), else `sheetFallback(wt)`; `copyFrame: true` copies the 18 px frame offsets verbatim (lines 486–490).

**Explicit cage entries in TILE_MAP — only 6 of the whole cage family:**
- Line 313 — `598: { key: 'v_598_lava_serpent_bowl', copyFrame: true },  // Lava Serpent Bowl`
- Line 316 — `316: { key: 'v_316_blue_jellyfish_jar', copyFrame: true },  // Blue Jellyfish Jar`
- Line 325 — `319: { key: 'v_319_ship_in_a_bottle', copyFrame: true },`
- Line 343 — `294: { key: 'v_294_zebra_swallowtail_butterfly_jar', copyFrame: true },`
- Line 344 — `291: { key: 'v_291_ulysses_butterfly_jar', copyFrame: true },`
- Line 345 — `292: { key: 'v_292_sulphur_butterfly_jar', copyFrame: true },`
- Line 346 — `289: { key: 'v_289_purple_emperor_butterfly_jar', copyFrame: true },`

**282, 505, 543, 521–527, 568–570, 317, 318, and all 64 lid cages (275, 285, 276–281, 296–299, 309, 310, 339, 358–364, 391–394, 413, 414, 532, 533, 538, 542, 544, 550, 551, 553–556, 558, 559, 582, 599–612, 619, 629, 632, 640, 643–645, 660, 710) are NOT in TILE_MAP** — they import purely through `SHEET_FALLBACK`, which resolves correctly because each `v_NNN` def has `sheet: NNN` and `framed: true` (→ `copyFrame: true`). Since sheets 0/3/5/389 are the only shared sheets, resolution is unambiguous for every cage id.

## 4. How `tiles.ts` defs drive rendering, and the CritterCage integration

Def fields consumed:
- `vanilla.sheet` → sprite file `vanilla/Tiles_${sheet}.png` (`VanillaTiler.ts` lines 640–650, via `atlas.ensureVImage(...)`; also `atlas.vmeta(sheet)` supplies `meta.sheet`/`grid`/`stride`).
- `vanilla.frame` — `'auto'` = 8-way autotile (`frameFor`, line 355/968), `'style'` = explicit 18 px frame, `'blend'` = BlendRules.
- `vanilla.fw`/`fh` — passed to `drawVanillaCell` and used for the broken-object frame reconstruction scan (lines 966–979) and the banded-layout re-map (lines 981–1000).
- `framed` — makes `ChunkCache` pass `frameX`/`frameY` through instead of recomputing.

Files:
- `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/VanillaTiler.ts`
- `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/ChunkCache.ts`
- `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/CritterCage.ts` (231 lines)

**VanillaTiler.ts integration points:**
- Line 16: `import { cageFamilyOf, drawCageCell } from './CritterCage';`
- Line 808: `export function drawVanillaCell(ctx, atlas, sheet, frame, fw, fh, st, x, y, type, same, px, py, fx, fy, trees?)` — the cage branch sits late in this function:
- Lines 1048–1057:
```ts
// ---- 小动物笼族（TileID.Sets.CritterCageLidStyle ≥ 0 的 64 张 + 360 金蝴蝶笼）：...
const cageFam = cageFamilyOf(sheet);
if (cageFam) {
  drawCageCell(ctx, atlas, cageFam, r, x, y, fx, fy, px, py, dyOff, r.img.height);
  return;
}
```
This dispatch is placed **before** the slope/half-brick branch (line 1060 onwards), so cages never enter slope rendering.
- Line 1186: `export const DRAW_Y_OFFSET: Record<number, number>` — the static `tileTop` table. Lines 1191–1201 already include `275: 2, 282: 2, 285: 2, 505: 2, 521–527: 2, 543: 2, 568: 2, 569: 2, 570: 2, 598: 2`; lines 1204–1217 add the remaining 63 lid cages (`276..710: 2`) with the comment "旧表只收 275/285 两张，其余 63 张缺顶 2px——配套 CritterCage.ts 的顶盖/帧行动画".

**ChunkCache.ts integration points:**
- Line 8: `import { cageAnimRate, cageFamilyOf } from './CritterCage';`
- Lines 192: `const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet] ?? (cageFamilyOf(sheet) ? cageAnimRate() : 0);` inside `advanceAnim()` (line 187) — rebake cadence.
- Lines 332–338, inside the per-tile bake:
```ts
} else if (cageFamilyOf(def.vanilla.sheet)) {
  // 小动物笼族（CritterCage.ts）：帧行偏移由 drawVanillaCell→drawCageCell

- Line ~341: the `drawVanillaCell(...)` call passing `def.vanilla.sheet/frame/fw/fh` and `st.frameX[i], fy`.

**CritterCage.ts internals:** `CAGE_FRAMES = 25` (line 25), `LID_STYLE` (lines 32–47, 64 entries — verbatim 1:1 of decompiled `TileID.cs:199`, which I verified at `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/TileID.cs:199`), `CAGE_FAMILY` (lines 66–142, **65 entries** = the 64 lid cages + 360 gold butterfly cage), `cageFamilyOf` (145), `cageSlot` (157), `cageFrameRow` (181), `cageAnimRate` (193), `drawCageCell` (205–231) which does the animated band row (`asy = r.sy + row * fam.step`), the 8 px top-row body shift, and the `vanilla/CageTop_${lid}.png` lid draw.

## Key finding: stale comment ③ in CritterCage.ts — the real gap

`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/render/CritterCage.ts` lines 20–21:

```
③ 282/505/543 鱼缸、598 熔岩鱼缸、568-570 妖精罐、521-527 蜻蜓罐、316-318
   水母笼不在本仓 tiles.ts，未收录。
```

This comment is **stale** — all of those ids (plus 289, 580, 620, 270, 271, 581, 660) are now defined in tiles.ts (see part 1), and they import fine via `SHEET_FALLBACK`. But they are **still absent from `CAGE_FAMILY`** (line 66: "本仓 tiles.ts 在库的全部 65 张笼表" — only the 64 lid cages + 360). Consequences for each of them:

- `cageFamilyOf(sheet)` returns `null` → `drawVanillaCell` falls through to the generic `style` path and draws only the **frame-0 band row** (no animation), and
- the ChunkCache line 332 branch never fires and `TILE_ANIM_RATE` has no entries for them → **no rebake registration** (they render as a frozen first frame).

In vanilla 1.4.5.6 (`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent.Drawing/TileDrawing.cs`, `GetTileDrawData`) these are the "water animal cage" group, all `tileTop = 2; Main.critterCage = true;` with a 36 px band:
- lines 5925–5935: `case 282: case 505: case 543:` → `addFrY = Main.fishBowlFrame[GetWaterAnimalCageFrame(...)] * 36`
- lines 5936–5944: `case 598:` → `Main.lavaFishBowlFrame[...] * 36`
- lines 5945–5953: `case 568: case 569: case 570:` → `Main.fairyJarFrame[...] * 36`
- lines 5954–5975: `case 288..295: case 360: case 580: case 620:` → `Main.butterflyCageFrame[num12, frame] * 36` (num12 = type-288, forced to 8 for 360/580/620)
- lines 5976–5989: `case 521..527:` → `Main.dragonflyJarFrame[typeCache-521, frame] * 36`
- lines 5990–6000: `case 316: case 317: case 318:` → `Main.jellyfishCageFrame[typeCache-316, GetSmallAnimalCageFrame(...)] * 36`

So if the intent is to animate these, they would slot into `CAGE_FAMILY` as `step: 36, slot: 'water'` (or `small` for jellyfish), `lid: -1`, using the existing `cageFrameRow` machinery. Note 270/271/581/572/660 use a *different* vanilla mechanism (`Main.tileFrame[typeCache] + x%N` horizontal `addFrX` with sprite flip, lines 5627–5654 and 5648–5696) rather than `GetWaterAnimalCageFrame`, so they are a separate case.

No files were modified.
````
